# Agents · A · part 2

909 entries, ac-fulfillment-gate to accounting-be. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **ac-fulfillment-gate**: AC fulfillment gate. Runs at priority 11.7 (after ac-validator at 11.5, before commit at 12). Resolves AC coverage via the shared ac_coverage_resolver module (accepts both the two-key ac_traceability form the generator emits and the legacy l2/l3/ac_path list form), then verifies AC YAML store fields (work_status, implemented_by, covered_by) are accurate and up-to-date before any commit is made. When verification fails but diff evidence exists, auto-fixes the YAML store fields (append-only, idempotent). Returns status: ok only when at least one AC was resolved and every resolved AC passes, or… ([urlmonitor/leafcutter-ai](https://github.com/urlmonitor/leafcutter-ai/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/ac-fulfillment-gate.md))
- **ac-implementer**: Implements an approved AgentChainer task contract in the working tree, within its scope, and explains each change. Use for the implement step of a task. ([hafizhrf/AgentChainer](https://github.com/hafizhrf/AgentChainer/blob/HEAD/agents/ac-implementer.md))
- **ac-independent-reviewer**: Reviews a finished change against its AgentChainer task contract in a fresh context (contract, diff and gate results only). Use for the review step of a task. ([hafizhrf/AgentChainer](https://github.com/hafizhrf/AgentChainer/blob/HEAD/agents/ac-independent-reviewer.md))
- **ac-reviewer**: Reviews a diff against the acceptance criteria in a Beads issue. Use after implementation work is complete and local gates pass, before pushing. ([defmethodinc/herb-embedded](https://github.com/defmethodinc/herb-embedded/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ac-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **ac-reviewer**: The evaluator's first moment: reads a card cold before anyone builds, judges whether its acceptance lines are testable, and writes the test script the tester will run after the ship. Use on every new or changed card, by an agent that did not write it and will not build it. ([hachej/boring-stack](https://github.com/hachej/boring-stack/blob/HEAD/plugins/boring-stack/agents/ac-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **ac-reviewer**: Reviews a branch or diff against its GitHub issue's acceptance criteria and the project's hard rules before a PR goes up. Use after finishing an issue and before /ship, or when reviewing someone else's PR. Catches leaked keys, unvalidated Gemini output, demo-path regressions, and ACs quietly marked done that aren't. ([Frankie813/hackathon-movies](https://github.com/Frankie813/hackathon-movies/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ac-reviewer.md))
- **ac-reviewer**: Fresh-context review of the branch diff against a spec's acceptance criteria. Read-only. ([teransarathchandra/agent-skills](https://github.com/teransarathchandra/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/setup/claude/agents/ac-reviewer.md))
- **ac-security-reviewer**: Reviews a planned AgentChainer task for security risks before it is implemented, read-only. Use in the parallel planning of large or security-relevant tasks. ([hafizhrf/AgentChainer](https://github.com/hafizhrf/AgentChainer/blob/HEAD/agents/ac-security-reviewer.md))
- **ac-test-fail-judge**: Decides, for each failing acceptance test of any feature, whether the implementation, the test, the translation or an outdated acceptance criterion is at fault, without seeing the implementation. Invoked only by the /speckit-ac-tests-analyze command with its fixed task message; never invoke it any other way. ([theyur/agentic-engineering-course-exam](https://github.com/theyur/agentic-engineering-course-exam/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ac-test-fail-judge.md) · 2 copies)
- **ac-test-planner**: Plans the tests that prove an AgentChainer task contract's acceptance criteria, read-only. Use in the parallel planning of large tasks. ([hafizhrf/AgentChainer](https://github.com/hafizhrf/AgentChainer/blob/HEAD/agents/ac-test-planner.md))
- **ac-test-writer**: Writes Reqnroll step definitions for one feature's locked Gherkin acceptance criteria, using only the spec and the public API from reference assemblies. Invoked only by the /speckit-ac-tests-write command with its fixed task message; never invoke it any other way. ([theyur/agentic-engineering-course-exam](https://github.com/theyur/agentic-engineering-course-exam/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ac-test-writer.md) · 2 copies)
- **ac-tracer**: The AC-evidence-trace tier of phase verification (read-only). Runs track-state spec-integrity, parses the result, and returns the per-AC grounding verdict. Fanned out in parallel with conductor:build-runner and conductor:test-runner before conductor:phase-checker (the synthesizer) consumes the fleet. ([AtaraxiaEpiphany/conductor](https://github.com/AtaraxiaEpiphany/conductor/blob/HEAD/agents/ac-tracer.md))
- **ac-triage**: Fast triage agent for /plan-feature workflow. Reads the AC store for the relevant component, compares the user's natural-language request against existing L0/L1 criteria text, and classifies the routing path as one of: strategic (new capability, no matching L1 parent), behavioral (adds to existing feature with a matching L1), technical (adds constraints to existing ACs), or covered (request fully covered by existing ACs). Returns a structured JSON decision immediately — no files are written by this agent. Model-pinned to Haiku tier for speed; must complete triage in &lt; 3s for a store of 200 AC… ([urlmonitor/leafcutter-ai](https://github.com/urlmonitor/leafcutter-ai/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/ac-triage.md))
- **ac-validator**: Final AC coverage gate. Validates all acceptance criteria are actually covered by the implementation before allowing commit. Reads the ticket ACs, the working diff, and test output, then produces a coverage verdict (ok / blocker / question). Use when: ticket-supervisor dispatches this agent at priority 11 (after pr-reviewer, before commit) to verify that every AC listed in the ticket has concrete evidence of both implementation and test coverage before the commit phase locks the worktree. ([urlmonitor/leafcutter-ai](https://github.com/urlmonitor/leafcutter-ai/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/ac-validator.md))
- **ac-verifier**: Independently RUN dual-source acceptance-criteria verification (Linear checkbox AC + PR body AC/checklist) for a ticket by fanning out one verify-only subagent per criterion, then synthesizing PASS/FAIL with evidence. Dedicated independent verifier only: never the implementer, project lead, or any code-writing agent for that PR. Has bash to run commands but does not edit code. ([FantasticDevHQ/pi-fleet](https://github.com/FantasticDevHQ/pi-fleet/blob/HEAD/agents/ac-verifier.md) · ★1)
- **ac-verifier**: Blind acceptance-criteria verifier for /new-idea Phase 6f. Reads the plan plus the git diff plus the current repo state and checks each AC against the implementation. Returns PASS/FAIL/MANUAL/DEFERRED per criterion with a top-line VERIFY VERDICT. Does not fix anything - audit only. Trusts only the plan and the diff, never implementer self-reports. ([androszr/mission-deck](https://github.com/androszr/mission-deck/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ac-verifier.md))
- **ac-verifier**: Independent verification that an implementation satisfies its acceptance criteria. Reads the ACs, the plan and the diff with fresh context, rules MET / PARTIAL / NOT MET per criterion with evidence, and returns a regroup brief for whatever is short. Never writes code and never fixes what it finds. ([chris59/emergent-claude-plugin](https://github.com/chris59/emergent-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/ac-verifier.md))
- **ac-verifier**: Specflow pipeline sub-agent. Read-only scoring of every acceptance criterion in a spec against a git diff, with path:line evidence (pass / partial / fail / manual). Never fixes or retries. Dispatched by the specflow orchestrator in Wave D (r1, then r2 and r3 on escalation) and by /specflow:verify. ([christie304/flow](https://github.com/christie304/flow/blob/HEAD/plugins/specflow/agents/ac-verifier.md))
- **ac-verifier**: Flow pipeline sub-agent. Read-only scoring of every acceptance criterion in a spec against a git diff, with path:line evidence (pass / partial / fail / manual). Never fixes or retries. Dispatched by the flow orchestrator in Wave D (r1, then r2 and r3 on escalation) and by /flow:verify. ([flowst8-dev/flowst8](https://github.com/flowst8-dev/flowst8/blob/HEAD/plugins/flow/agents/ac-verifier.md))
- **ac-verifier**: Maps acceptance criteria to implementation evidence, tests, and scope creep. Use during review when a diff, PR, ticket, or story includes numbered ACs. ([HoangNguyen0403/agent-skills-standard](https://github.com/HoangNguyen0403/agent-skills-standard/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/ac-verifier.agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **ac-verifier**: Use as the final gate before a build (or a build phase) is called done. Route here after the build and verify-acs have run and the builder claims every acceptance criterion for the phase is met, or whenever someone is about to tick off, close, or ship against docs/prd/acceptance-checklist.md. This is the independent evidence check, not a builder or fixer. Do NOT route here mid-build, for writing tests, or for open-ended "is my code good" reviews. ([jiffi-co/claude-plugins](https://github.com/jiffi-co/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/builder-kit/agents/ac-verifier.md))
- **ac-verifier**: Mechanical gate for ROADMAP acceptance criteria. Runs `cmd:` and `invariant:` ACs plus the Harness Build, Test, Lint, and Invariants commands against the current working tree or a worktree, and reports pass/fail per command. No edit tools. Does not judge behavior; `probe:` ACs belong to @validator. Used for per-task AC checks and per-phase integration checks. ([JonFermin/vibecoding](https://github.com/JonFermin/vibecoding/blob/HEAD/agents/ac-verifier.md))
- **ac-verifier**: Independent acceptance-criteria verifier for factory station S10. Given only a story's acceptance criteria, the diff and the project's test commands, it re-runs the tests and returns pass, fail or not-verifiable for each criterion, with evidence. It never changes code. ([Nandan-krishnamurthy/ai-software-factory](https://github.com/Nandan-krishnamurthy/ai-software-factory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ac-verifier.md))
- **ac-verifier**: Use to verify a feature's acceptance criteria and report a per-AC pass/fail status. The verification method is set by ac_verification_mode: observe the running app (browser/API) under runtime-assisted, run the test suite under tests, or read code under code-only or off. Use proactively after implementation when a spec's AC items need verification; runtime-assisted requires the app to be running. ([webmint/mintEnvoy](https://github.com/webmint/mintEnvoy/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ac-verifier.md) · 2 copies)
- **ac-verifier**: Independent verifier of a specforge spec's acceptance criteria. Give it the spec id and the commit sha to verify, never the author's reasoning. It runs every criterion's verify target itself, writes the evidence file, and returns a results table. It never fixes anything. ([zalmb8/specforge](https://github.com/zalmb8/specforge/blob/HEAD/agents/ac-verifier.md))
- **ac-writer**: Product Team acceptance-criteria seat - fills each story's Scenarios row with the PRD scenario ids that slice satisfies, and reports any slice needing a criterion no scenario covers. Use ONLY from /5-decompose with the PRD path and the story files; it never invents a requirement or a scenario. ([jmanuelrosa/agent-catalog](https://github.com/jmanuelrosa/agent-catalog/blob/HEAD/bundles/product-team/agents/ac-writer.md))
- **ac2-security-reviewer**: Reviews AC2 protocol code (frame parser, CRC-16-CCITT, HMAC-SHA-256, replay protection) against the IRS and SR-* requirements. Use proactively on any diff that touches UART framing, crypto, or the gateway bridge. Not yet relevant — the parser does not exist in the repo yet (as of Phase 1a). ([oruccakir/AegisCore](https://github.com/oruccakir/AegisCore/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ac2-security-reviewer.md))
- **aca-report-agent**: Writes the results summary of an Android cleanup run from the CLI's own numbers: before/after RAM and storage, what was removed, turned off and changed, what was skipped or rolled back and why, and how to undo it. ([willcoliveira/android-cleanup-agent](https://github.com/willcoliveira/android-cleanup-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/aca-report-agent.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **aca-risk-judge-agent**: Independent risk judge. Reviews the approved change list of an Android cleanup run right before it is applied, and vetoes items that look risky for this user (a feature they said they use, a high-risk change without a clear reason, an app that looks like one of their main apps). It can only remove items from the approved set; it never approves or adds anything. ([willcoliveira/android-cleanup-agent](https://github.com/willcoliveira/android-cleanup-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/aca-risk-judge-agent.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **academic**: Academic Vern - Needs more research. Cites sources, considers prior art, wants peer review. Use for thorough analysis and evidence-based decisions. ([jdonohoo/vern-bot](https://github.com/jdonohoo/vern-bot/blob/HEAD/agents/academic.md) · ★15)
- **academic**: The peer-reviewed-evidence lens for STORM research. Use when you need what the actual studies and data show — including conflicting findings, effect sizes, and the quality of the evidence base. Spawned in parallel by the storm-research and multi-perspective-scan skills, one of five expert personas. Writes its analysis to the run workspace as academic.md. ([HirogaKatageri/hirokata-claude-marketplace](https://github.com/HirogaKatageri/hirokata-claude-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/research/agents/academic.md) · ★6 · 3 copies)
- **academic**: Dedicated academic subagent for course material ingestion, note CRUD, flashcard creation, and validation under special/academia/. ([polyipseity/information](https://github.com/polyipseity/information/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/academic.agent.md) · ★5)
- **academic**: Academic research lens for Storm Council: grounds the decision in peer-reviewed evidence and examines methodological limits. Use in Storm Council Stage 3 (evidence) and Council Mode. ([huguryildiz/storm-council](https://github.com/huguryildiz/storm-council/blob/HEAD/agents/academic.md) · ★1)
- **Academic Deliverable Verifier**: Read-only academic deliverable checks for grammar, evidence, citations, and output agreement; not drafting, customization authoring, or acceptance. ([E-G-C/dude](https://github.com/E-G-C/dude/blob/HEAD/library/packs/academic-writing/agents/dude-pack-academic-writing-deliverable-verifier.agent.md) · ★1)
- **Academic Paragraph Polisher**: Use when polishing academic Chinese paragraphs for flow, coherence, and clarity, especially math education writing, thesis sections, and literature review text. - **Academic Writer**: Use when: 需要在本地笔记和文献基础上，撰写、润色、修改或排版理工科（如物理、计算机）类型的学术文章、中英文论文摘要等。 ([MoMoJee/MoMoJeeObsidian](https://github.com/MoMoJee/MoMoJeeObsidian/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/academic-writer.agent.md) · ★5)
- **Academic Writer**: Agente de redação acadêmica Qualis A1 (ABNT/APA/Vancouver, IMRAD). ([MarceloClaro/opencode-ecosystem-core](https://github.com/MarceloClaro/opencode-ecosystem-core/blob/HEAD/agents/academic_writer.md) · ★3)

- **Academic Writer**: Coursework writing specialist for academic assignments, discussion posts, APA-supported responses, and reviewable quantitative companions at the level of the assigned readings. - **academic-advisor**: Academic research and tutoring consolidated agent. - **academic-anthropologist**: Expert in cultural systems, rituals, kinship, belief systems, and ethnographic method — builds culturally coherent societies that feel lived-in rather than invented ([RabbitAI-Lab/rabbit-plugins-experts](https://github.com/RabbitAI-Lab/rabbit-plugins-experts/blob/HEAD/plugins/academic-anthropologist/agents/academic-anthropologist.md))

- **academic-design-reviewer**: Academic-publication design reviewer for the Arduino PBL program documents — the master document (Arduino_PBL_Program.md) and both overview documents (Arduino_PBL_Program_Overview.md and Arduino_PBL_Program_Overview_he.md). Reads the markdown source and the md-to-pdf config (CSS) together. Does NOT modify files directly. - **academic-economist**: Critical academic economist (macro, labour, public finance, inequality) who referees research ideas, model specifications and draft papers from a non-economist author. - **academic-editor**: Use this agent when you need rigorous academic critique at any phase of the research lifecycle — literature review evaluation, manuscript review, or pre-submission editorial decisions. - **acceptance-gatekeeper**: Independent acceptance reviewer and release gatekeeper. Given a merged-candidate PR/diff plus its originating spec (requirement, PRD, or a decision ledger of ADRs + acceptance gates), decides whether the change is FIT TO ENTER THE TRUNK — not by re-reading every line (peers cover that) but by answering the owner's question: does this deliver exactly what was asked, honour the locked decisions, and carry no unrequested scope? - **Acción 01 — Investigar Requerimiento**: Pipeline action 01/17. Use to analyze a new product idea or requirement from a business and user perspective before any technical work begins. Produces a deep understanding of the problem: who has the problem, why they have it, what they're doing today instead, and what success looks like. Triggers on: "acción 01", "investigar requerimiento", "action 01", "investigate requirement", "entender el problema", "contexto del req". ([MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype](https://github.com/MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/product.action.investigar-requerimiento.agent.md))
- **Acción 02 — Análisis Funcional**: Pipeline action 02/17. Use to define the complete functional scope of the product: what it does, what it doesn't do, key user flows, and functional requirements with acceptance criteria. Builds on the investigation from Action 01. Triggers on: "acción 02", "análisis funcional", "action 02", "functional analysis", "scope funcional", "qué hace el sistema", "functional requirements", "casos de uso". ([MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype](https://github.com/MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/product.action.analisis-funcional.agent.md))
- **Acción 03 — Revisión Técnica del Requerimiento**: Pipeline action 03/17. Use to validate that the requirement is technically sound, identify technical unknowns and risks early, and confirm that the proposed stack (.NET 8, n8n, Gemini, Azure SQL, Next.js) is appropriate for the functional scope. Triggers on: "acción 03", "revisión técnica", "action 03", "technical review", "viabilidad inicial", "stack apropiado", "technical feasibility of requirement". ([MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype](https://github.com/MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/product.action.revision-tecnica-req.agent.md))
- **Acción 04 — Viabilidad Técnica**: Pipeline action 04/17. Use to produce a comprehensive technical feasibility assessment covering architecture fit, integration complexity, performance expectations, estimated technical effort, and a final viability verdict with level of confidence. Builds on Action 03 red flags. Triggers on: "acción 04", "viabilidad técnica", "action 04", "technical feasibility", "factibilidad técnica", "es técnicamente viable", "cuánto cuesta técnicamente", "technical viability report". ([MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype](https://github.com/MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/product.action.viabilidad-tecnica.agent.md))
- **Acción 05 — Riesgos e Impacto**: Pipeline action 05/17. Use to identify, classify, and plan mitigation for all technical and business risks of the project. Also assesses impact on existing systems, teams, and processes. Triggers on: "acción 05", "riesgos", "action 05", "risk analysis", "análisis de riesgos", "impacto del sistema", "risk register", "risk matrix", "riesgos técnicos", "riesgos de negocio". ([MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype](https://github.com/MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/product.action.riesgos-impacto.agent.md))
- **Acción 06 — Shape Up del Alcance**: Pipeline action 06/17. Use to apply the Shape Up methodology to define the project appetite, carve the scope into bets with fixed time, and identify the rabbit holes to avoid. Produces a shaped pitch ready for the betting table. Triggers on: "acción 06", "shape up", "action 06", "shaping", "apetito", "appetite", "pitch del proyecto", "scope fijo", "fixed time variable scope", "betting table". ([MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype](https://github.com/MrAlexisCord/chatbot-prototype/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/product.action.shape-up.agent.md))
- **Acción 07 — Estrategia y Alcance del MVP**: Pipeline action 07/17. Use to define the MVP strategy: what the minimum viable product includes, the value hypothesis, positioning against alternatives, and the success metrics to validate the MVP.
